```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO26-017-ShipShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeChapter11Dash1Applicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Chapter 11-1 applicability must be recorded as exactly one boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:polarCodeChapter11Dash1Applicable ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:voyagePlanPresent ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "When Chapter 11-1 applies, the voyage plan must be present."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:intendedVoyageHazard ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Each intended voyage hazard must be recorded as a string."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:voyagePlanCoverage ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "When Chapter 11-1 applies, the voyage plan must cover every intended voyage hazard."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    SELECT $this ?hazard
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:intendedVoyageHazard ?hazard .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:voyagePlanCoverage true .
                        }
                    }
                """ ;
                sh:message "Every intended voyage hazard must have voyage-plan coverage equal to true when Chapter 11-1 applies."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:polarCodeChapter11Dash1Applicable ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?

```turtle
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive "2012-01-01"^^xsd:date ;
                sh:maxExclusive "2019-01-01"^^xsd:date ;
                sh:message "A ship contracted from 1 January 2012 through 31 December 2018 must have a valid construction contract date."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:in ( nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2010 ) ;
                sh:message "Ships contracted from 1 January 2012 through 31 December 2018 must identify the 2010 Ice Class Regulations as the applicable regulation edition."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:or (
                    [
                        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                        sh:maxExclusive "2012-01-01"^^xsd:date
                    ]
                    [
                        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                        sh:minInclusive "2019-01-01"^^xsd:date
                    ]
                ) ;
                sh:message "A ship outside the 1 January 2012 to 1 January 2019 applicability interval must have a construction contract date establishing that it is outside the interval."
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

```turtle
            ]
        ]
```

```turtle
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a CP propeller, pitchValueKnown must be explicitly true or false." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:propellerType ?type .
                FILTER (?type = "CP"^^xsd:string)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:pitchValueKnown ?known .
                    FILTER (?known = true || ?known = false)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [

            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
```

```turtle
gen:TRF084ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;

        sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A single propeller type is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
                sh:hasValue "CP"^^xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;

                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:message "For a CP propeller with known pitch, the MCR bollard-condition pitch must be supplied in metres."
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
                sh:hasValue "CP"^^xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:pitchValueKnown ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrFreeRunning ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrBollard ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "For a CP propeller with unknown pitch, the MCR bollard-condition pitch must equal 0.7 times the MCR free-running pitch within a relative tolerance of 1e-6 and an absolute fallback of 1e-9 metres." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:propellerType "CP"^^xsd:string ;
                              nltl:pitchValueKnown false ;
                              nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrFreeRunning ?freeQuantity ;
                              nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrBollard ?bollardQuantity .
                        ?freeQuantity qudt:numericValue ?free ;
                                      qudt:unit unit:M .
                        ?bollardQuantity qudt:numericValue ?actual ;
                                      qudt:unit unit:M .
                        BIND(0.7 * ?free AS ?expected)
                        BIND(IF(ABS(?expected) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                                ABS(?expected) * 0.000001,
                                0.000000001) AS ?tolerance)
                        FILTER(ABS(?actual - ?expected) > ?tolerance)
                    }
                """
            ] ;
            sh:message "For a CP propeller with unknown pitch, both free-running and bollard-condition MCR pitch values are required."
        ]
        [
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The non-CP propeller alternative is applicable only when propeller type is not CP." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:propellerType ?propellerType .
                        FILTER(STR(?propellerType) = "CP")
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
```

```turtle
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [

                        sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]

                sh:message "Flange width must be at least five times the net web thickness." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2_042_ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2_042_StructuralMemberShape>
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2_042_StructuralMemberShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:structuralMember ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:netWebThickness ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:flangeWidth ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Flange width must be at least five times the net web thickness." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:netWebThickness ?thicknessQuantity ;
                      nltl:flangeWidth ?flangeQuantity .
                ?thicknessQuantity qudt:numericValue ?thickness .
                ?flangeQuantity qudt:numericValue ?flange .
                FILTER (?flange < (5 * ?thickness))
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
Adequate thermal protection shall be provided for all persons on board, taking into account the intended voyage, the anticipated weather conditions (cold and wind) and the potential for immersion in polar water, where applicable.
Normalized requirement:
Every person on board shall have assigned thermal-protection equipment supported by an adequacy approval covering intended voyage, anticipated cold and wind, and applicable polar-water immersion.
Required inputs: Persons-on-board manifest, assigned equipment, and adequacy evidence

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-064> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:personOnBoard ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must record the number of persons on board."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Every person on board must have an assigned thermal-protection equipment assignment." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:personOnBoard ?personCount .

                {
                    SELECT $this (COUNT(DISTINCT ?person) AS ?assignedPersonCount)
                    WHERE {
                        ?assignment a rdf:Statement ;
                                    rdf:predicate nltl:assignedThermalProtection ;
                                    rdf:object true ;
                                    rdf:subject ?person .
                        ?person ?shipMembershipPath $this .
                    }
                    GROUP BY $this
                }

                FILTER (?assignedPersonCount < ?personCount)
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:intendedVoyage ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The intended voyage must be recorded for thermal-protection adequacy."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:anticipatedCold ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Anticipated cold must be recorded for thermal-protection adequacy."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:anticipatedWind ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Anticipated wind must be recorded for thermal-protection adequacy."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:immersionPotential ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The applicable potential for immersion in polar water must be recorded."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thermalProtectionApprovalStatus ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceState ;
        sh:message "Thermal-protection adequacy approval must have approved status and cover the intended voyage, anticipated cold and wind, and applicable polar-water immersion."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The approved thermal-protection adequacy evidence must be ship-specific and cover the ship's intended voyage, anticipated cold and wind, and applicable polar-water immersion." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:thermalProtectionApprovalStatus nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
                      nltl:intendedVoyage ?shipVoyage ;
                      nltl:anticipatedCold ?shipCold ;
                      nltl:anticipatedWind ?shipWind ;
                      nltl:immersionPotential ?shipImmersion .

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?evidence a rdf:Statement ;
                              rdf:subject $this ;
                              rdf:predicate nltl:thermalProtectionApprovalStatus ;
                              rdf:object nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
                              nltl:intendedVoyage ?evidenceVoyage ;
                              nltl:anticipatedCold ?evidenceCold ;
                              nltl:anticipatedWind ?evidenceWind ;
                              nltl:immersionPotential ?evidenceImmersion .

                    FILTER (
                        ?evidenceVoyage = ?shipVoyage &&
                        ?evidenceCold = ?shipCold &&
                        ?evidenceWind = ?shipWind &&
                        ?evidenceImmersion = ?shipImmersion
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
Ships shall comply with SOLAS regulation V/22.1.9.4, irrespective of the date of construction and the size, and have a clear view astern. On ships which cannot comply with these requirements, arrangements acceptable to the Administration shall be provided to achieve a level of visibility that is equivalent to these requirements.
Normalized requirement:
SOLAS_V_22_1_9_4_compliance_status = compliant AND clear_view_astern_status = compliant, OR administration_approved_equivalent_visibility_arrangement_present = true.
Required inputs: SOLAS/visibility compliance evidence or approved equivalent arrangement

Original source text:
Ships constructed on or after 1 January 2017, ice strengthened in accordance with chapter 3, shall have either two independent echo-sounding devices or one echo-sounding device with two separate independent transducers.
Normalized requirement:
IF construction_date >= '2017-01-01' AND ship_ice_strengthened = true, THEN independent_echo_sounding_device_count >= 2 OR (echo_sounding_device_count >= 1 AND independent_transducer_count >= 2).
Required inputs: Construction date, ice-strengthening status, and echo-sounding equipment topology

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO081Shape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction date is required to determine applicability of the equipment requirement."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Ice-strengthening status is required to determine applicability of the equipment requirement."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO081NonApplicableByDateShape
        gen:IMO081NonApplicableByIceStatusShape
        gen:IMO081AlternativeOneShape
        gen:IMO081AlternativeTwoShape
    ) .

gen:IMO081NonApplicableByDateShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:maxExclusive "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date
    ] .

gen:IMO081NonApplicableByIceStatusShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:in ( false )
    ] .

gen:IMO081AlternativeOneShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minInclusive "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:in ( true )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:independentEchoSoundingDeviceCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive 2 ;
        sh:message "An applicable ship shall have at least two independent echo-sounding devices."
    ] .

gen:IMO081AlternativeTwoShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minInclusive "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:in ( true )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:echoSoundingDeviceCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable ship shall have at least one echo-sounding device."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:independentTransducerCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive 2 ;
        sh:message "An applicable ship shall have at least two independent transducers."
    ] .

```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 250
Batch: BATCH_25
Requirement ID: TRF-081
Source family: TRAFICOM
Verification mode: DIRECT_CALCULATION
Source document: Ice Class Regulations and the Application Thereof
Source revision: TRAFICOM/68863/03.04.01.00/2021
Clause / section / page: 6.5.2.1-6.5.2.2 / Maximum ice thrust on propeller Tf and Tb for open and ducted propellers / 37
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-081-20260915T134124284246Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The maximum forward and backward ice thrusts are T_f = 1.1*F_f [kN] (6.17) and T_b = 1.1*F_b [kN] (6.18). The design thrust along the propeller shaft line must be calculated using the formulae below. The greater value of the forward and backward direction loads shall be taken as the design load for both directions. Factors 2.2 and 1.5 take account of the dynamic magnification resulting from axial vibration. In a forward direction, T_r = T + 2.2*T_f [kN] (6.19). In a backward direction, T_r = 1.5*T_b [kN] (6.20).
Normalized requirement:
Compute T_f_kN = 1.1*F_f_kN and T_b_kN = 1.1*F_b_kN. Compute forward_design_thrust_T_r_kN = hydrodynamic_bollard_thrust_T_kN + 2.2*T_f_kN and backward_design_thrust_T_r_kN = 1.5*T_b_kN. Set design_thrust_T_r_kN = max(forward_design_thrust_T_r_kN, backward_design_thrust_T_r_kN) for both directions.
Required inputs: Ff_kN, Fb_kN, and hydrodynamicBollardThrust_kN; the separate TRF-082 record covers the Table 6-8 fallback when bollard thrust is unknown

```turtle
                $this nltl:forwardBladeForce ?forwardBladeForceNode ;
                      nltl:backwardBladeForce ?backwardBladeForceNode ;
                      nltl:hydrodynamicBollardThrust ?bollardThrustNode ;
                      nltl:forwardIceThrust ?forwardIceThrustNode ;
                      nltl:backwardIceThrust ?backwardIceThrustNode ;
                      nltl:forwardPropellerShaftDesignThrust ?forwardDesignThrustNode ;
                      nltl:backwardPropellerShaftDesignThrust ?backwardDesignThrustNode ;
                      nltl:propellerShaftDesignThrust ?designThrustNode .

                ?forwardBladeForceNode qudt:numericValue ?forwardBladeForce .
                ?backwardBladeForceNode qudt:numericValue ?backwardBladeForce .
                ?bollardThrustNode qudt:numericValue ?bollardThrust .
                ?forwardIceThrustNode qudt:numericValue ?forwardIceThrust .
                ?backwardIceThrustNode qudt:numericValue ?backwardIceThrust .
                ?forwardDesignThrustNode qudt:numericValue ?forwardDesignThrust .
                ?backwardDesignThrustNode qudt:numericValue ?backwardDesignThrust .
                ?designThrustNode qudt:numericValue ?designThrust .

                BIND (1.1 * ?forwardBladeForce AS ?expectedForwardIceThrust)
                BIND (1.1 * ?backwardBladeForce AS ?expectedBackwardIceThrust)
                BIND (?bollardThrust + 2.2 * ?expectedForwardIceThrust AS ?expectedForwardDesignThrust)
                BIND (1.5 * ?expectedBackwardIceThrust AS ?expectedBackwardDesignThrust)
                BIND (IF (
                    ?expectedForwardDesignThrust >= ?expectedBackwardDesignThrust,
                    ?expectedForwardDesignThrust,
                    ?expectedBackwardDesignThrust
                ) AS ?expectedDesignThrust)

                FILTER (
                    ABS(?forwardIceThrust - ?expectedForwardIceThrust) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedForwardIceThrust) + 0.000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?backwardIceThrust - ?expectedBackwardIceThrust) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedBackwardIceThrust) + 0.000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?forwardDesignThrust - ?expectedForwardDesignThrust) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedForwardDesignThrust) + 0.000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?backwardDesignThrust - ?expectedBackwardDesignThrust) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedBackwardDesignThrust) + 0.000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?designThrust - ?expectedDesignThrust) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedDesignThrust) + 0.000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this

                      nltl:backwardBladeForce ?fbNode ;
                      nltl:hydrodynamicBollardThrust ?hNode ;
                      nltl:forwardIceThrust ?tfNode ;

                      nltl:forwardPropellerShaftDesignThrust ?trfNode ;

                      nltl:propellerShaftDesignThrust ?trNode .

                ?fbNode qudt:numericValue ?fb .
                ?hNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?tfNode qudt:numericValue ?tf .
                ?tbNode qudt:numericValue ?tb .
                ?trfNode qudt:numericValue ?trf .
                ?trbNode qudt:numericValue ?trb .
                ?trNode qudt:numericValue ?tr .

                BIND(1.1 * ?ff AS ?tfExpected)
                BIND(1.1 * ?fb AS ?tbExpected)
                BIND(?h + 2.2 * ?tfExpected AS ?trfExpected)
                BIND(1.5 * ?tbExpected AS ?trbExpected)
                BIND(IF(?trfExpected >= ?trbExpected, ?trfExpected, ?trbExpected) AS ?trExpected)

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?tf - ?tfExpected) >
                    (0.000001 * IF(ABS(?tfExpected) > 1, ABS(?tfExpected), 1))
                    ||
                    ABS(?tb - ?tbExpected) >
                    (0.000001 * IF(ABS(?tbExpected) > 1, ABS(?tbExpected), 1))
                    ||
                    ABS(?trf - ?trfExpected) >
                    (0.000001 * IF(ABS(?trfExpected) > 1, ABS(?trfExpected), 1))
                    ||
                    ABS(?trb - ?trbExpected) >
                    (0.000001 * IF(ABS(?trbExpected) > 1, ABS(?trbExpected), 1))
                    ||
                    ABS(?tr - ?trExpected) >
                    (0.000001 * IF(ABS(?trExpected) > 1, ABS(?trExpected), 1))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:backwardBladeForce ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;

            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [

        sh:node [

            sh:property [

            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:forwardIceThrust ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;

            sh:property [

                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;

            ]

    ] ;
    sh:property [

            sh:property [

    ] ;
    sh:property [

        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;

            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;

                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:backwardPropellerShaftDesignThrust ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerShaftDesignThrust ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Ice thrust and propeller shaft design thrust values must satisfy the TRF-081 formulas." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:forwardBladeForce ?ffNode ;
                      nltl:backwardBladeForce ?fbNode ;
                      nltl:hydrodynamicBollardThrust ?tNode ;
                      nltl:forwardIceThrust ?tfNode ;
                      nltl:backwardIceThrust ?tbNode ;
                      nltl:forwardPropellerShaftDesignThrust ?frNode ;
                      nltl:backwardPropellerShaftDesignThrust ?brNode ;
                      nltl:propellerShaftDesignThrust ?rNode .

                ?ffNode <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/numericValue> ?ff .
                ?fbNode <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/numericValue> ?fb .
                ?tNode <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/numericValue> ?t .
                ?tfNode <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/numericValue> ?tf .
                ?tbNode <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/numericValue> ?tb .
                ?frNode <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/numericValue> ?fr .
                ?brNode <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/numericValue> ?br .
                ?rNode <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/numericValue> ?r .

                BIND (1.1 * ?ff AS ?expectedTf)
                BIND (1.1 * ?fb AS ?expectedTb)
                BIND (?t + 2.2 * ?expectedTf AS ?forwardLoad)
                BIND (1.5 * ?expectedTb AS ?backwardLoad)
                BIND (IF(?forwardLoad >= ?backwardLoad, ?forwardLoad, ?backwardLoad) AS ?designLoad)

                FILTER (
                    ABS(?tf - ?expectedTf) >
                    IF(ABS(?expectedTf) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                       ABS(?expectedTf) * 0.000001,
                       0.000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?tb - ?expectedTb) >
                    IF(ABS(?expectedTb) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                       ABS(?expectedTb) * 0.000001,
                       0.000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?fr - ?designLoad) >
                    IF(ABS(?designLoad) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                       ABS(?designLoad) * 0.000001,
                       0.000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?br - ?designLoad) >
                    IF(ABS(?designLoad) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                       ABS(?designLoad) * 0.000001,
                       0.000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?r - ?designLoad) >
                    IF(ABS(?designLoad) * 0.000001 > 0.000000001,
                       ABS(?designLoad) * 0.000001,
                       0.000000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```
